- **Components** ([Release v4.26.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.26.3)): The connector line between steps in a horizontal stepper no longer runs through the step names; it now runs between the step numbers and shows in forced-colors mode (#411)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.5)): A persisted partition job step whose observer was later unsubscribed or retired threw `Argument type must be an interface` while activating, so it failed and was retried continuously, flooding the kernel log and burning CPU. It now activates and reports the disconnection the job already handles.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.4)): Appending a batch for several event sources where some events carry named tags now keeps the registered event source of every event in the batch, so registered-source metadata and definition validation apply to those batches and to unit-of-work commits, as they already did for single appends (#4603)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.11)): The Stage Host now returns every matching read model for a list query instead of only the first. An unkeyed list query no longer advertises an `id` parameter, and a keyed list query takes its parameter under its modeled name.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.11)): Rendered read models no longer mark a list query's `by` property as the read-model key. Only single-result lookups set `[Key]`.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.11)): An authorized unkeyed query now gets its generated authorization policy registered, so the `[Authorize]` attribute on it no longer points at a missing policy. An authorization rule that needs a query argument the query does not have is refused with `STAGE-ESM-015`. (#261)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.3)): `EventSourceId<T>` now turns numeric, date and time values into event source ids, and parses them back, with the invariant culture, so the same identity names the same stream on every machine. A `decimal` id of `123.45` is `"123.45"` under `nb-NO` too, instead of `"123,45"` (#4651)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.3)): Typed event source ids over `DateOnly`, `DateTimeOffset`, `TimeOnly` and `TimeSpan`, and over concepts that wrap numbers or dates, can now be converted back from an untyped `EventSourceId` instead of throwing
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.2)): Several applications registering into the same event store each sent a "full set" that retired every client-owned projection not in its own set, including the other applications'. They retired and re-registered each other's projections in a loop, which saturated the projections manager and made `Register` time out. A full set now only retires projections the same application registered; the client identifies itself with its entry assembly name, and a full set from a client that does not identify itself retires nothing.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.10](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.10)): Stage tables that navigate on a row click now carry the selected row's identity in the address (`#/WorkItemDetails?workItemId=...`), restore the selection from a deep link, and clear it from the address when the selection is cleared. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.10](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.10)): Tables fed by a `data ... by <parameter>` declaration now read through that declaration's query with the selected identity, so a details screen shows only the selected item's rows, ignores a delayed response for a previously selected item, and reads nothing while no item is selected. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.10](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.10)): Native command forms on a screen navigated by a parameter now start with that parameter in the same-named command input, so Rename and AddComment submit for the selected work item; inputs with no matching parameter, such as a new comment's identity, stay required user input. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.10](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.10)): Switching the Scene theme now repaints the page body background and text color, not only the shell. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.9](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.9)): Authored command actions in `/stage/scene` now include modeled command schemas, required metadata, and field descriptors so native command forms can render and validate inputs before submission. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.9](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.9)): Stage marks missing command metadata separately from intentionally parameterless commands, keeping unsafe command actions fail-closed instead of submitting empty payloads. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Ante** ([Release v1.2.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Ante/releases/tag/v1.2.11)): The wizard's form and step header sat flush against the card edge, because their padding was written for PrimeReact's stepper classes that Cratis Components 4 no longer renders. The card also touched the window edges on narrow screens.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.37.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.37.1)): Reconnecting clients no longer stall the kernel by re-registering unchanged read models: registration now compares definitions by value and only persists and pushes new or changed read models, instead of rewriting every read model on every attempt.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.8](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.8)): Stage now serves authored command forms as native runtime command-form elements with modeled field descriptors and JSON schema metadata, so required inputs are available before submit instead of falling back to empty command bodies. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.8](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.8)): Stage now attaches command routes to native command-form elements as well as action buttons, so form submits and command actions resolve to the same backend route. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.7)): Stage tables now publish selected rows as exact component outputs, including stable ids that contain dots, so named component bindings resolve the selected identity without falling back to a global selection. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.7)): Dependent Stage queries now clear and stay suppressed when bound arguments are null or missing, ignore stale delayed responses after reselection, and avoid fetching unfiltered routes during selection clear. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.7)): Native Stage command forms now fail closed when command field and schema metadata are absent, while still allowing explicitly parameterless commands. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **AI** ([Release v7.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.16.0)): Subscription pool calls stop failover after any native function invocation attempt, including failed or in-flight attempts, so automatic retries cannot repeat uncertain tool effects.
- **AI** ([Release v7.16.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.16.0)): Protected provider credentials that have not been released for use now fail with an actionable reconfiguration error instead of reaching outbound transports as encrypted envelopes.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.5)): Native Stage command forms now keep dirty input values across equivalent scene reloads and layout-only rebinds by stabilizing the Arc command descriptor identity. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.5)): Native Stage command forms now honor explicit empty JSON Schema required lists as optional input and visibly block unsupported object, array, and fractional field-width metadata instead of silently coercing it. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.4)): Authored Scene command actions now receive their Stage command API routes, so native command controls for modeled commands are exposed instead of disabled. (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.4)): Stage route metadata now maps modeled command names to the supported runtime command endpoints used by native command forms. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.3)): Native Stage command forms now post valid Arc command values and refresh scoped queries while preserving validation feedback and dirty input through context refreshes. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **cli** ([Release v3.40.2](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.40.2)): `cratis run` now defaults to the Stage 4.49.2 sandbox image, which includes the browser runtime data fix for authored Scene applications. (part of #284)
- **cli** ([Release v3.40.2](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.40.2)): `cratis render` now consumes the matching Stage 4.49.2 renderer package while keeping the bundled Screenplay 4.105.0 corpus and query-specification support. (part of #284)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.2)): Stage now serves `/stage/scene`, `/stage/routes` and `/stage/locales` for authored screen compositions that contain guarded screen interactions the runtime can fail closed. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.2)): Guarded screen actions are removed from the runnable Scene payload instead of blocking the whole Stage shell, so the authored screens still load while unsupported guarded behavior stays unavailable. (part of #237)
- **AI** ([Release v7.15.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.15.1)): Restricted checkout workers no longer receive shared repository-cache mounts or cache environment settings in Kubernetes and Docker. Ordinary workers retain their existing cache behavior. Upgrade the `Cratis.AI` package alongside worker images to close the alternate cached-source access path.
- **cli** ([Release v3.40.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.40.1)): `cratis render` now emits authored Scene screen compositions from the compiled source documents instead of replacing them with the generated backend command/query fallback surface. (part of #284)
- **cli** ([Release v3.40.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.40.1)): Unsupported guarded screen actions are omitted from the runnable Scene payload and reported as `STAGE-SCENE-ACTION-001` warnings, preserving fail-closed behavior while keeping the rest of the authored app surface. (part of #284)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.35.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.35.2)): Open alert incident gauges can now be disabled with `Cratis__Chronicle__Alerts__IncidentMetricsEnabled=false` on every silo, followed by a restart. This stops both gauge production and background incident sampling without disabling alerts or other telemetry; existing installations remain enabled by default. See [incident metrics configuration](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/blob/main/Documentation/hosting/configuration/open-telemetry.md#open-alert-incident-metrics).
- **Stage** ([Release v4.49.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.49.1)): Rendered applications and Stage sessions refuse screens with guarded actions (`STAGE-SCENE-ACTION-001`) or guarded `on click`, `on double click` and `on select` interactions (`STAGE-SCENE-INTERACTION-001`) until the Scene runtime can evaluate them, instead of showing an always-visible button that sends no command or dropping the interaction's branches. The Scene contract and screen-composition planning still carry these constructs, and a Stage host reports the refusal as unsupported instead of failing at startup. (part of #209)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.6](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.6)): Story investigation workers can check out every scheduled private repository into owner-qualified paths using separate clone-read credentials, without enabling automatic pushes or push-token refresh. Workers fail before launching the agent if any checkout fails or broad GitHub authentication is supplied.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.48.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.48.3)): Read models in the Chronicle Workbench keep updating after the Stage host reconnects to Chronicle. The host's Chronicle client no longer auto-registers an empty projection set on every connect, which retired the projections Stage had registered, and Stage re-registers its event types, read models and projections on each reconnect. (part of #228)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.48.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.48.2)): A concept declared `secret scope namespace` or `secret scope global` renders `[Encrypted(EncryptionScope.Namespace)]` or `[Encrypted(EncryptionScope.Global)]` even when the `complianceDetails` option is off, instead of falling back to subject scope. The option now gates only the free-text reason and details. Concepts without a scope, or with subject scope, render as before. (part of #250)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.48.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.48.1)): Native Stage command forms now wait for registered routes before rendering and keep the Arc CommandForm hook lifecycle stable during route loading. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.48.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.48.1)): Native Stage command forms preserve dirty values while query refreshes update screen data and render manual field geometry with unequal, resizable columns. (part of #236) (part of #237)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.105.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.105.0)): Screen composition corpus admission now keeps UI/backend ESM refusals while allowing the canonical screen list-query shapes to bind and execute without weakening authorization or protected refusal outcomes. (part of #536)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.4)): Kubernetes workers configured for the `batch` node pool now prefer nodes already hosting ephemeral runners or managed AI workers across namespaces, rather than spreading them. Other node pools retain their existing placement behavior.
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.4)): Kubernetes worker pods now carry `cluster-autoscaler.kubernetes.io/safe-to-evict: "false"` to protect running work from Cluster Autoscaler scale-down independently of PodDisruptionBudgets. Explicit deletion and node failures remain possible.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.61.0)): A scenario is generated only when every command and event property it needs can be stated from the specification. Scenarios that rely on generated values such as `CustomerId.New()`, leave a property unset, or expect a `null` value are now left out with `SP0039`, so the document binds under Screenplay's complete-fixture rules instead of failing with `SP0056` (#3078)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.61.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.61.0)): Authoring-only scoped documents no longer both import and declare the same read model, or import one they never declare. The import is now decided from the declarations the scoped document actually emits (#3064)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.35.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.35.1)): Projection futures read back from storage keep the event store and namespace they belong to, so a projection with nested children no longer fails every event with "missing from the event store '[NotSet]'" when its futures are served from another silo (#4656)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.3)): The Screenplay model review skill routes Chronicle CLI questions to `cratis-chronicle-cli-operations` instead of a skill that does not exist (#542)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.3)): The Lens skill routes executing a command from backend code to the right skill, and the React page dialog reference points to the step that registers confirmation and busy-indicator dialogs (#542)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.3)): The Screenplay specifications skill states that the reference runner executes clock, trigger and capture actions from ESM v6 (#542)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.3)): The identity skill states in `SKILL.md` that a resolved tenant is never an authorization decision (#542)
- **AI** ([Release v7.14.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.14.3)): The release-notes skill reads a repaired release body back before reporting the repair as done (#542)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.58.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.58.3)): The generated Screenplay model keeps `identifier` and the `for` destination on a command whose handler returns an event together with a separate response, `(TEvent, TResponse)`, including through `Task` and `ValueTask`. Such commands no longer report `SP0013`/`SP0051`, and their specifications no longer run as unsupported identity allocation. A response typed as an event source identifier, `object`, an interface, or a union that can carry an identity still leaves the destination unstated. (#3069)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.58.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.58.3)): A command scenario that only asserts the command was rejected, such as `IsValid.ShouldBeFalse()` or `ShouldHaveValidationErrors()`, is now generated as a bare `then error` that binds and matches the rejection without asserting a message, instead of `then error ""`. (#3046)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.41.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.41.0)): Rendered scoped projections fill the read model's identifier property from the projection key, so identifier queries and joins that rely on it match Screenplay. (part of #117)
- **cli** ([Release v3.37.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.37.1)): `cratis prologue interpret` no longer sends capture evidence to a machine-wide model when the project's `cratis-prologue.json` sets `llm.enabled: false`; `--no-llm` forces local, heuristics-only interpretation; and the command names the provider, destination and where its configuration came from before sending anything, rejecting an endpoint it cannot name (#241)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.58.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.58.2)): Embedded Screenplay generation now preserves the project's compiler features and interceptor namespaces, including those used by OpenAPI source generators, instead of reporting compilation errors for otherwise valid generated code. Existing `SourceCompilation.Create` callers remain compatible.
- **Stage** ([Release v4.39.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.39.0)): Unsupported layouts now remain visible as unresolved Scene components instead of silently substituting a default shell. (part of #236)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.89.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.89.1)): The `@cratis/screenplay-language` package builds and publishes again. Its runtime size check now catches a dependency bundled into the runtime, and no longer fails as the language grows
- **Stage** ([Release v4.38.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.38.1)): Rendered command handlers set `$context.occurred` from the time Arc received the command instead of the clock at handling time, so an event and the policy that authorized it see the same time, and a fixed `TimeProvider` controls it in tests. A command without a receipt time is refused instead of falling back to the current time. (#238)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.58.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.58.1)): A generated Screenplay specification is left out, reported with `SP0039` naming the missing properties, when the Arc scenario asserts only some properties of an event, instead of producing a specification that does not bind. (#3072)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.87.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.87.1)): The TypeScript compiler reports the reaction refusal and redelivery diagnostics PLAY0540, PLAY0541, PLAY0542, PLAY0543 and PLAY0544 the same way the C# compiler does, so Monaco and VS Code workspace files show every refusal and redelivery problem the command-line check reports (#521)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.85.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.85.1)): Merging a folder, or collapsing it into one document, keeps identical module and feature header comments written in different files, in file order, and a trailing comment on the owning file's header stays on that line (#517)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.84.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.84.1)): PLAY0515 also rejects `@sensitive` concepts used as a command identifier, an explicit `for` destination or an event-source identifier, since an event source id can be neither encrypted nor erased (#384)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.84.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.84.1)): The concepts guide and the `@pii` hover no longer claim that Chronicle applies `@pii` and `@sensitive` rules automatically (#384)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.5)): The event model board shows a specification's effective typed example values, `when redelivered` actions and `then no events` expectations, and keeps showing refusal outcomes (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.4)): The TypeScript compiler reports PLAY0391 and PLAY0478 like the C# compiler, and Monaco and VS Code no longer drop shared diagnostics such as PLAY0514, PLAY0515 and PLAY0453 (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.4)): The grammar reference documents `not` in policies, and the documentation agrees on which inputs count as reachable from navigation and forms (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.3)): The Invoicing sample demonstrates populating a form from the selected row (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.2)): The `@cratis/screenplay-language` package builds and publishes again; its size check had rejected the runtime once timeline read checks and event-first reaction resolution were both included
- **Arc** ([Release v22.57.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.57.1)): The Screenplay generator no longer states validation rules as unconditional when they only run under a condition: inside a named rule set other than `default`, after an early `return` or `throw` in the validator, under `When`/`Unless`, or in a `DependentRules` or `Otherwise` callback. Such rules are left out and reported with `SP0016`, for command and concept validators. (#3045)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.82.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.82.1)): The event model board shows a specification's generated values, the values `then returns` expects, and the read models `then` asserts are absent, instead of dropping them (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.7)): When an event and an application trigger share a name, a reaction's `for` resolves to the event everywhere: validation, the dependency graph, personal-data checks, rename and find-references, MCP references, and hover and go-to-definition in Monaco and VS Code (#439)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.6)): VS Code highlights `depends on` declarations, and the Invoicing sample shows a declared module dependency (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.5)): The Monaco editor colours a document's `numbers` preamble as a keyword instead of as a plain identifier (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.5)): The grammar, AST authoring and workspace transport references describe exact numeric mode: the `numbers` preamble, per-file mode agreement, bounded decimals and the canonical `ExactNumber` form (part of #495)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.34.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.34.2)): A quarantined observer now stays quarantined until an operator clears it or it receives a fresh subscription. Before, a watchdog check, a catch-up or replay job finishing, or the event store subscription check could silently resume it. Completed work still records its progress, so clearing the quarantine resumes from where that work stopped, and for a subscribed observer it also resumes paused jobs and retries failed partitions. [Ending quarantine](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/blob/main/Documentation/hosting/configuration/observers.md) lists exactly what ends a quarantine. (#4440)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.32.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.32.0)): Reaction and type descriptions containing `<`, `>`, `&` or line breaks no longer produce broken XML documentation or code. (#178)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.4)): The MCP server's initialization guidance and the `open-workspace` tool description now say how the server finds a model: nested `.play` files in the client's project root, then `Source` or `src`, then a new `Screenplay` folder, and `Documents/Screenplay` in the home folder when there is no client root (part of #495)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.3)): PLAY0515 now rejects personal-data values typed directly in a reaction's trigger clause as reaction destinations, for registered and undeclared triggers, in both compilers and the editors (#442)
- **cli** ([Release v3.33.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.33.0)): Shell completion no longer lists every subcommand of a command group twice, and no longer offers the global output options for `cratis screenplay mcp`.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.2)): The MCP event model board keeps its Board and Map controls and a usable dependency map when the board cannot be read or drawn, and a failure in one view no longer takes down the other (#477)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.2)): A selected dependency map node or edge, and its details, stay selected across edit refreshes in VS Code while the item still exists, including while a filter hides it (#478)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.81.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.81.1)): Printing a Screenplay document keeps large integers and small fractions as written, instead of switching to scientific notation, in event payloads and stream routes (#476)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.30.0)): Stage status reports the runtime engine actually selected, including `--engine=` overrides and the default `eventmodel` engine; it previously reported an engine only when the environment variable set it to `semantic`. (part of #23)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.28.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.28.0)): Rendered command handlers append each produced event to the event source named by its `for`, instead of always using the command identifier, and the rendered specifications check each event on that same event source. Productions without `for` are unchanged. (#187)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.34.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.34.1)): Reactors and other observers with tag, event source type or event stream type filters no longer receive events their filters exclude when they catch up, replay or recover a failed partition. Events appended before the observer registered are now filtered the same way as events delivered live, and the observer moves past excluded events without counting them as handled (#4529)
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.26.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.26.0)): A command, its aggregate, events, read model and endpoints sharing one flat feature namespace are split into slices by artifact instead of failing generation with `GEN0007` (#81)
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.25.0)): Enum concepts no longer capture a same-named enum that was rejected, and enum values the Screenplay compiler cannot parse are reported instead of generated
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.25.0)): Types without a concept, HEAD and OPTIONS endpoint effects, query identifiers and timeout triggers are kept in generated models
- **Screenplay.CritterStack** ([Release v0.25.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.CritterStack/releases/tag/v0.25.0)): Fetched streams are linked only to targets that can be proven
- **Arc** ([Release v22.55.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.55.0)): A generated query states `by <parameter>` only when the parameter matches a property of the read model it returns; otherwise the parameter is a `filter`. (#3039)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.79.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.79.0)): A target repeated on a module or feature that is written twice in one document is now reported as a repeat (`PLAY0555`), as it is across files, and the unknown-directive message lists `depends on`. (part of #416)
- **cli** ([Release v3.30.1](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.30.1)): `cratis screenplay mcp` started with `--project-root` or `--project-root-env`, as `cratis ai install` registers it, or with a `.cratis/ai.json` that sets no model root, keeps serving a folder that already holds Screenplay workspace state, so applied identities and interrupted-write recovery are no longer lost when the model lives in a nested folder. When several folders between the project and the model hold state, the one nearest the project is used and the others are reported on standard error; an interrupted write in another of those folders, or workspace metadata that is a link or has the wrong file type, stops startup with a message naming the folder. (#268)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.76.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.76.1)): `module` completion no longer offers `layout` and `dialog`; it offers `screen template` and `dialog template`, which the language has.
- **cli** ([Release v3.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.30.0)): In the embedded MCP server, moving a feature or slice earlier among its siblings in a typed edit is no longer refused, and merging a folder model back into one document keeps comments written on `authorize` statements.
- **cli** ([Release v3.30.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.30.0)): `cratis render` rejects a mo
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.75.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.75.0)): The Monaco tokenizer no longer throws on a `generated` property written without `identifier`.
- **Arc** ([Release v22.52.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.52.0)): A validation message no longer attaches to the preceding rule when the validator it follows cannot be read. (part of #2887)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.52.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.52.0)): The embedded event model board shows event tag values instead of their syntax, and does not ask for generated values as command inputs. (part of #2887)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.4)): A rejected reactor or reducer registration no longer breaks later calls on the same connection. Before, a keep-alive or another call sent while the failed stream was closing could fail with "Reading is already in progress". (#4537)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.4)): A rejected reactor or reducer registration now ends the call with an error that the client sees. Before, the call could end as successful while the client kept waiting. (#4537)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.4)): A reactor or reducer registration with a missing event type is rejected with a clear error instead of failing inside the kernel. (#4537)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.73.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.73.3)): The Monaco and VS Code editors now treat `readmodel` as a construct inside a slice, and highlight, complete and document every construct the language has (`reducer`, `dialog`, `form`, `contribute`, `theme`, `ui`, `behavior`, `specification` and more).
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.73.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.73.2)): Merging a folder model back into one document no longer drops comments written on `authorize` statements. (#412)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.73.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.73.1)): The Screenplay MCP server again keeps an existing workspace's identities and interrupted-write recovery when it picks its model folder itself (no explicit path and no configured root) and the model lives in a nested folder such as `Models/` or `Source/`. If the offered folder, or a folder between it and the model, already holds `.screenplay` state, that folder is used, as before 4.67. (#451)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.73.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.73.1)): When more than one of those folders holds `.screenplay` state, the one nearest the offered folder is used and `open-workspace` reports the competing folders, including when opening fails; nothing is migrated. If you created state in the model folder with 4.67 or later and want to keep using it, open that folder explicitly. An interrupted write recorded in a competing folder blocks reading and writing until you open that folder and recover it. (#451)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.3)): Replaying an observer while it is finishing a catch-up no longer drops the replay or returns an empty job id. The replay waits for the hand-over to complete, then starts, and `Replay` returns the id of the replay job that was actually started. This also covers automatic replays after a projection, reducer, reactor or webhook definition changes. (#4514)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.3)): Resuming a stopped replay job no longer stalls until the call times out. A resume from outside the observer waits until the observer has entered replay before it starts processing. It is refused while the observer is disconnected or quarantined, so the job stays stopped. (#4514)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.3)): `Replay` on projections, reactors and reducers documents that it returns no job id when the observer cannot replay from its current state (disconnected or quarantined). (#4514)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.72.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.72.0)): The MCP server merges a workspace's files in file-path order, matching folder compilation on disk; it previously merged them in an order derived from document identifiers. (#434)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.2)): Event content with unsigned 64-bit integers above `long.MaxValue` (for example `ulong.MaxValue`) can be appended, read back, revised and redacted with MongoDB storage. This includes values in arrays and named primitive types. Before, the append failed. (#4588)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.2)): MongoDB read models store and read unsigned 64-bit values above `long.MaxValue` without wrapping them to negative numbers. Values written by earlier versions still read back correctly. (#4588)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.2)): SQL storage keeps unsigned 64-bit event content exact instead of converting it through a floating-point value. (#4588)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.1)): Global seeds registered with `SeedEvents` are now applied to namespaces created afterwards, for example a tenant namespace ensured after startup. Namespaces created before this release are not seeded retroactively. (#4547)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.1)): The in-process test host in `Cratis.Chronicle.XUnit.Integration` registers the kernel's system event types at startup and after every reset, so tests that create additional namespaces work against this release. (part of #4547)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.0)): Clients that encode contracts with proto3 semantics (TypeScript, Go, Kotlin, Elixir, Python) can register reactors that are not replayable and webhooks that are not replayable or not active. `ReactorDefinition` gains `IsNotReplayable`, and `WebhookDefinition` gains `IsNotReplayable` and `IsInactive`. These plain booleans default to `false`, and a `true` value turns the setting off even when `IsReplayable` or `IsActive` is omitted. Existing clients and the existing fields keep working unchanged. (#4394)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.33.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.33.0)): `ObserverInformation.IsReplayable` is now always sent, so proto3 clients read the correct value from `GetObservers` instead of `false` for every observer. (#4394)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.70.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.70.0)): Moving a feature or slice earlier among its siblings in a typed workspace edit now prints it at its new position instead of after the last sibling, so the move is no longer refused. (part of #413)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.69.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.69.1)): Report undeclared events in projection removals and capture appends, and missing read-model properties in projection mappings, in both compilers. (#378)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.69.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.69.1)): Reject `@pii` concepts used as event-source identifiers or typed command and reaction destinations, with guidance to use a surrogate identifier. (part of #384)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.69.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.69.0)): The imports and folders documentation now says that the MCP App looks for another importing root only when the folder has no `application.play`, and that VS Code compiles only what an importing document imports when no `application.play` sits above it. (part of #413)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.68.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.68.4)): Include reactions in the MCP event model board’s added and removed summaries and current and proposed counts. (#379)
- **cli** ([Release v3.28.5](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.28.5)): `cratis ai install` and `cratis ai update` now install scripts with their execute bit, so hook scripts such as `.cratis/ai/hooks/scripts/cratis-quality-gate.sh` can be run directly. A file is executable when the corpus ships it as executable or it starts with a shebang; execute is granted only to those who can already read it, and `update` repairs existing installations and removes execute bits from managed files that are not scripts. When an installed script must become executable but another user owns it, or the file system refuses every mode change on it, the update stops before changing any file and says so; scripts the file system otherwise leaves non-executable are listed under `warnings`. (#251)
- **cli** ([Release v3.28.4](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.28.4)): Screenplay validation and MCP use Screenplay 4.66.1, and `cratis version` displays the bundled compiler version in text and JSON output. (#242)
- **cli** ([Release v3.28.4](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.28.4)): `cratis screenplay validate <file>` follows the root file's imports, counts imported documents and reports diagnostics against their source files. (#244)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.30.5](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.30.5)): Unicode service hosts are canonicalized the same way as incoming internationalized host names, including decomposed, fullwidth and other compatibility forms. Declarations whose mapping would turn into a port or other authority delimiter are rejected at startup, and plain ASCII host names parse as before. (#173)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.30.5](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.30.5)): Activity-timeout documentation states the supported range of 1 to 2,147,483,647 milliseconds. (#173)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.68.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.68.3)): Draw the MCP App and VS Code event model boards in authored declaration and import order, respecting final file placement and alphabetical glob matches without changing executable model bytes or identities. (#411)
- **Orleans** ([Release v1.15.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Orleans/releases/tag/v1.15.3)): Trace context now propagates across grain calls in silos hosted with `AddCratisOrleans`, while tenant context remains separate from activity baggage. (#47)
- **Screenplay.Generation** ([Release v0.18.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay.Generation/releases/tag/v0.18.1)): Preserve compiler warning codes, messages, severity, and source positions in generated Screenplay definitions, including successful generation. (#64)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.68.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.68.2)): The reactions, contexts and MCP reference pages describe how ESM v7 generated values and command responses behave. (#420)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.10.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.10.2)): Append constraint violations use the messages declared on the client's configured event types, including batch and transactional appends. (#94)
- **Chronicle.Elixir** ([Release v3.10.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Elixir/releases/tag/v3.10.2)): Connection checks tolerate delayed trust-store loading and failed-session startup under load. (#70)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.68.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.68.1)): The event model board shows the values each specification sets and asserts, its error messages and its caller, using the updated board package. (#409)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.4](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.4)): Waiting for an append to complete no longer waits for, or reports failed partitions from, internal kernel reactors such as pattern capture that are not subscribed in the target namespace. Projections, webhooks, event-store subscriptions and client observers remain included in completion waits, even before subscribing or while disconnected. (#4492)
- **Components** ([Release v4.25.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.25.2)): Pinching on a trackpad or touch device no longer falls through to the browser's own page zoom when the gesture event carries no pointer coordinates, as can happen with Safari gesture events. Canvas now falls back to the last pointer position and then the canvas centre, so the pinch zooms the canvas instead (Cratis/StudioIssues#476)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.7)): An application whose authentication or authorization handlers are registered as scoped no longer fails at startup when the discovery endpoints require authentication, which is the default outside Development, and services that only support asynchronous disposal are disposed correctly during that startup check (#3032)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.6)): Paged MongoDB query results no longer fail when the query pipeline materializes them for read-model interception. (#2966)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.6)): Route parameter names containing regex metacharacters are treated literally by GET and QUERY requests. (#3014)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.6)): Generated command properties such as `Origin` no longer collide with the command runtime's backing fields. (#2978)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.6)): Core documentation now describes discovery as anonymous only in Development by default, with authentication required elsewhere. (#2965)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.50.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.50.6)): Chronicle command examples now use nullable projected-state parameters and handle missing state explicitly without triggering ARC0006. (#2940)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.3)): An observer no longer stays behind the tail when an event is appended while its catch-up job is finishing. A new catch-up starts for that event instead of waiting for the finishing job, and overlapping catch-ups share a single job. (#4548)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.3)): Observers created without stored state no longer share their catching-up, replaying and in-flight partition sets, so one observer's catch-up no longer makes the others drop live events. (#4558)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.3)): A reducer batch that fails part-way is retried from the start of the batch, so events folded before the failure are no longer skipped. (#4540)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.3)): Webhooks in event stores other than the System store now keep their authorization and their updates to event types, target URL and headers, and adding, updating or removing a webhook now fails when one of its events could not be appended. (#4567)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.3)): MongoDB: a projection write that arrives while a catch-up's final bulk flush is in progress is now written instead of dropped, so the read model no longer stays behind an observer that reports the event as handled. (#4598)
- **Components** ([Release v4.25.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Components/releases/tag/v4.25.1)): `CommandDialog` no longer replaces a render-function child with its mapped result, so a render-prop component such as a paged-results helper inside a command dialog no longer throws `children is not a function` (#396)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.30.3](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.30.3)): Canonical OIDC identities now resolve correctly during sign-in notification, subject linking, and legacy invitation completion, using the authenticated provider or session scheme instead of the identity's authentication type. (#122)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.30.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.30.2)): Replacement invitation pages can now display HTML-encoded invited and asserted email addresses during validated invitation selection and completion, helping users identify a wrong-account sign-in without exposing addresses outside the invitation flow. (#133)
- **AI** ([Release v7.11.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.11.1)): The quality gate checks the repository's own projects instead of the AI corpus packages that `cratis ai install` placed in it, runs a gate in every package a change touches, and never reports success when a package it should have checked was skipped (#487)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Global encrypted values can provision their keys with MongoDB; the reserved global key store no longer produces an invalid MongoDB database name. (#4549)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Protected numbers and booleans are released with the type their schema declares instead of as JSON strings, including inside arrays, for erased values and for enums stored by name. (#4550)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Compliance metadata declared on a dictionary's value schema (`additionalProperties`) is now applied to every dictionary value: values are encrypted, erased with their subject and refused after erasure. (#4551)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Array elements that are themselves classified collections are encrypted, released and erased as a whole, while objects with declared properties keep per-member protection, so data already stored that way still releases. (#4552)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Event-type migrations transform plaintext instead of ciphertext, so split, combine and map work on `[PII]` values, and each target generation is protected for the original subject. (#4456)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Revised event content and the revision request are protected with the original event's subject before they are stored. (#4525)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.2)): Projection folds, immediate projections and `GetInstanceById` keep the subject each protected value was written under, so values whose subject differs from the read-model key are released correctly. (#4561)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.66.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.66.2)): The event model board shows each specification's command once instead of twice. (#408)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.1)): MongoDB: removing a child read model through a join (`RemovedWithJoin`) now matches child identifiers named `id` or `Id`, including children in nested collections, and skips documents where the collection is missing or not an array. SQL removes children in nested collections the same way. (#4538)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.1)): Projection Preview keeps the properties of a read-model schema it infers from registered events, including arrays of objects, references and nullable `anyOf`/`allOf` values. The inferred schema saved with the projection now contains them too. (#4539)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.1)): Projections that subscribe to all events alongside explicit `from` declarations now see every event in replay, read-model snapshot history and Preview, also when the all-events subscription uses a custom key. (#4562)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.1)): Registering a projection with an open-schema child joined by an explicit identifier no longer fails with a `NullReferenceException`. (#4564)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.32.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.32.1)): MongoDB: a read model whose identifier property is spelled `ID` (or any casing other than `id`/`Id`) keeps its key when read back. The same spelling on SQL is tracked in https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/issues/4590. (#4536)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.66.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.66.1)): Compiler diagnostics, editor hints, the event-model board, MCP tool descriptions and the documentation no longer promise a future executable model version for generated values and responses, operations and systems, event sources and streams, or exact numbers. They now say these constructs are not admitted by any supported executable model version yet; binding still refuses them with `PLAY0268`. (part of #309)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.4.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.4.0)): A strict scenario now rejects a root-level join-removal event as unsubscribed, matching the kernel, instead of refusing the whole projection (part of #38)
- **release-action** ([Release v1.4.2](https://github.com/Cratis/release-action/releases/tag/v1.4.2)): A run that loses the version race to a concurrent run now fails its post step instead of reporting success. When the release for its tag already exists for another commit, the step fails naming that commit, so jobs that need the release job do not publish under a version that belongs to someone else; re-running the run releases it under the next version. See [Concurrent runs](https://github.com/Cratis/release-action/blob/main/README.md#concurrent-runs) (#178)
- **AI** ([Release v7.9.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.9.2)): The Claude Code hooks run each guard script through `bash`, so the write, store-mutation and PR-body guards and the quality gate keep enforcing when an install or checkout has lost the scripts' execute bit (#480)
- **AI** ([Release v7.9.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.9.2)): The store-mutation guard describes `cratis chronicle jobs resume` as resuming a stopped job; a failed job cannot be resumed (#466)
- **AI** ([Release v7.9.2](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.9.2)): The PR-body guard checks the pull request a command actually edits: it honours a command-local `GH_REPO`, resolves a PR URL, `--repo` or `GH_REPO` target before an unknown working directory, keeps checking later commands on the same line, and lets `gh` pick the repository when none is named (#472)
- **cli** ([Release v3.27.2](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.27.2)): `cratis ai install` and `cratis ai update` keep an existing MCP configuration's own indentation, nesting and line endings when they add or remove a server, so `.vscode/mcp.json` and the other JSON and Codex TOML configurations no longer get whitespace-only lines, and removing a server gives back the file as it was (#248)
- **AI** ([Release v7.9.1](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.9.1)): Claude subscriptions authenticated with a long-lived `claude setup-token` token now report their 5-hour and weekly windows. The usage endpoint refuses those tokens with 403 because they lack the `user:profile` scope. Capacity is now read from the rate limit headers of a one-token Messages request, including when the subscription is already over its limit.
- **AI** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Claude subscription limit messages such as "You've hit your weekly limit" are recognized as rate limits, so a pool fails over to another member instead of failing the work.
- **AI** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): A provider that hits a limit is parked until the reset the limit states, not for a flat hour, so it is no longer retried hourly while its weekly limit lasts.
- **AI** ([Release v7.8.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.8.0)): Pool completions skip members whose capacity says they cannot start work, and `AIProviderPoolDispatcher` skips rate-limited members.
- **AI** ([Release v7.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.7.0)): Translation has one meaning across rules, skills and prompts: outside data becomes your own facts. (#490)
- **AI** ([Release v7.7.0](https://github.com/Cratis/AI/releases/tag/v7.7.0)): The Screenplay skills no longer say that reacti
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.22.8](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.22.8)): Nested derived types — direct properties, arrays, and collections or dictionaries whose element type is a derived-type family — are now written with their type identifier. (#1149)
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.22.7](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.22.7)): Serializing and deserializing objects with null fields, null array elements and null map values no longer throws, and explicit nulls are preserved. (#1148)
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.22.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.22.6)): OpenTelemetry SDK disablement now applies to tracing, metrics, and logging added after `WithCratis`, including when its configuration differs from the host configuration. (#1147)
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.22.6](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.22.6)): HTTP OTLP endpoints now preserve query strings when appending trace, metric, and log paths. (#1147)
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.31.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.31.11)): A client whose observer registration failed about 44 times in a row (for example during a long kernel outage) no longer gets stuck throwing `OverflowException: TimeSpan overflowed` on every retry. Registration now backs off up to its one-minute cap and recovers once the kernel is reachable again.
- **Chronicle** ([Release v19.31.11](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle/releases/tag/v19.31.11)): In a multi-silo kernel, a Cratis exception thrown across silos (such as `ClientIsNotConnected` during reducer registration) now reaches the caller with its type and message. Before, the response failed to serialize (`Serialization and deserialization of 'System.Reflection.MethodBase' instances is not supported`) and the caller hung until it timed out.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.63.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.63.2)): The Screenplay MCP server reads the folders a host shares with it from the `roots` array that the MCP schema defines. It expected a different field, so with Claude Desktop the startup handshake failed, Claude restarted the server between calls, and every call after `open-workspace` failed with "Open a workspace first" or a rejected fallback folder.
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.63.2](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.63.2)): A failure while handling a notification is now written to the server's log instead of being sent back as an error with a null id, which made hosts drop the connection. The session stays open.
- **Fundamentals** ([Release v7.22.5](https://github.com/Cratis/Fundamentals/releases/tag/v7.22.5)): JavaScript `DateOnly`, `TimeOnly` and `TimeSpan` now reject out-of-range values such as `2024-13-45`, `25:99:00` and `00:60:00` as C# does, and `TimeOnly` keeps the full seven-digit fraction so map keys that differ below a millisecond no longer collide (#1160)

## Removed

- **cli** ([Release v3.53.0](https://github.com/Cratis/cli/releases/tag/v3.53.0)): The `CLI-RENDER-015` refusal of navigation contributions that declare `id`, `icon`, `presentation`, `group` or `destination outlet|dialog|external`: Stage 4.73.0 honours them, so `cratis render` now renders them and writes them into the scene. (part of #284)
- **Arc** ([Release v22.65.3](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.65.3)): `IIdentitySource` and `IdentitySource`, `CommandScenario.Generate(...)` and `QueryScenario.Generate(...)`, and the two-argument `EventSourceValuesProvider` constructor are removed. They were added in v22.64.0, but nothing could use them: an unnamed queue of `Guid` values can't pin named generated values of other types, handlers that create ids with `New()` or `Guid.NewGuid()` bypass it, and no Cratis code depends on it. Named, typed generated values will replace them. (#3125)

## Security

- **Arc.Go** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc.Go/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): Upgrade golang.org/x/net to v0.60.0 to fix GO-2026-6603, GO-2026-6610, GO-2026-6611, GO-2026-6612 and GO-2026-6617 (#79)
- **Arc.Go** ([Release v0.1.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc.Go/releases/tag/v0.1.1)): Build and test with the latest Go patch release, which fixes the same issues in the standard library (#79)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.9.1](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.9.1)): Upgrade golang.org/x/net to v0.60.0 to fix GO-2026-6603, GO-2026-6610, GO-2026-6611, GO-2026-6612 and GO-2026-6617 (#100)
- **Stage** ([Release v4.31.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Stage/releases/tag/v4.31.0)): A claim compared with a Uuid matches only the Uuid's canonical lower-case text. A query argument that reaches the policy as an empty, upper-case or otherwise non-canonical string no longer matches a claim, so a caller holding an empty claim value is denied. (part of #57)
- **AuthProxy** ([Release v2.30.4](https://github.com/Cratis/AuthProxy/releases/tag/v2.30.4)): Access tokens cached for a backend are no longer reused after its address changes during a configuration reload, even when the session and audience are unchanged. (#177)

## Deprecated

- **Arc** ([Release v22.74.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Arc/releases/tag/v22.74.0)): `ProtectedIdentityAnnotations` (in `Cratis.Arc.Screenplay.Emission`) is obsolete; the generator no longer uses it, and it will be removed in a future major version. (#3096)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.100.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.100.0)): `@pii`, `@sensitive` and `sensitive` (including `sensitive reason`) still compile, with an information diagnostic and a repair for the line or the whole document that keeps every reason (part of #557)
- **Screenplay** ([Release v4.99.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Screenplay/releases/tag/v4.99.0)): An opaque `where` guard on `on click`, `on double click` or `on select`. A guard that reads as an item condition reports a warning with a repair that rewrites it as a `when` branch. Other guards report information. `where` on event, change and application triggers is unchanged (#447)
- **Chronicle.Go** ([Release v0.9.0](https://github.com/Cratis/Chronicle.Go/releases/tag/v0.9.0)): `readmodels.DecisionProtectedModel` and `readmodels.DecisionProtectionMetadata` are no longer returned by decision-read admission and will be removed in a future release. (part of #34)
